853 entries, microbial-taxonomy-abundance-analysis to microshift-ci:prow-job. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **microbial-taxonomy-abundance-analysis**: Use when you have final ASV abundance tables from a DADA2/dadasnake amplicon run (e.g. mock communities ERR777696 or PRJNA626434) and a known expected taxon composition, and need to test whether relative read abundances reflect true taxon proportions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbial-taxonomy-abundance-analysis))
- **microbial-vs-chemical-exposome-pathway-comparison**: Use when you have an exposure–transcriptome network (e.g., MetaNet co_net/multi1 objects) and need to compare KEGG/GO over-representation of genes associated with different exposure types (microbial vs chemical). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbial-vs-chemical-exposome-pathway-comparison))
- **microbiologist**: Think and work like an expert Microbiologist. Use when a task calls for Microbiologist judgment. Reasons from culturability limits, CFU/MPN enumeration, selective media, DADA2/QIIME2 16S ASVs (SILVA/GTDB), and shotgun metagenomics (Kraken2, MetaPhlAn, HUMAnN); treats plate-count anomaly, compositional stats pitfalls, kit contamination, and index hopping as first-class failure modes. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/tree/HEAD/scientific-agents/microbiologist/skills/microbiologist) · ★255 · 2 copies)
- **microbiologist**: Use when a task needs the judgment of a Microbiologist — identifying an organism from a culture (biochemical panel, MALDI-TOF, or 16S rRNA sequencing), investigating a contamination source in a sterile or food-production environment, interpreting antimicrobial susceptibility testing against CLSI breakpoints, back-calculating a contamination timeline from growth kinetics, or judging whether an environmental-monitoring excursion is a genuine control-failure signal. - **microbiome**: Read before you analyze amplicon or shotgun microbiome data (ASVs, taxonomy, diversity, differential abundance). The data is compositional, thus usual tests and RNA-seq methods give false results. It gives the correct method for each design and the rules for rarefaction and zeros. ([inflexa-ai/inflexa](https://github.com/inflexa-ai/inflexa/tree/HEAD/skills/microbiome) · ★38)
- **microbiome**: Analyze 16S rRNA and shotgun microbiome data for community composition and diversity. ([honeyvig/skillsbench](https://github.com/honeyvig/skillsbench/tree/HEAD/shared_workspace/phylogenetic-tree-construction/environment/skills/microbiome))

- **microbiome-community-visualization-phyloseq**: Use when quality-filtered 16S rRNA amplicon reads have been processed into OTU (97% identity, mothur) or ASV (DADA2 denoising) tables with taxonomy assignments, and taxonomic composition visualizations are required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-community-visualization-phyloseq))
- **microbiome-compositional-data-analysis**: Use when evaluating which DA method controls FDR at a nominal level (e.g. 5%) while maintaining power on compositional microbiome count data, or when reproducing simulation-based method comparisons across balanced and unbalanced group designs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-compositional-data-analysis))
- **microbiome-count-normalization**: Use when raw OTU/feature count tables need normalization prior to ordination, BSS, or differential abundance analysis, and competing normalization methods must be ranked on a dataset such as the global gut microbiota data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-count-normalization))
- **microbiome-count-normalization-css-tmm-quantile**: Use when a merged MetaPhlAn or QIIME OTU/BIOM taxonomy table needs rescaling across samples before GLM, Random Forest, or SVM model building, or when comparing how normalization choices affect classifier accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-count-normalization-css-tmm-quantile))
- **microbiome-count-normalization-undersampling-correction**: Use when calling differentially abundant features (OTUs, species, or other taxa) across two or more sample groups from sparse, undersampled microbial count matrices where library-size normalization biases dominate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-count-normalization-undersampling-correction))
- **microbiome-curation**: Skill for curating microbiome-related pathophysiology in the dismech knowledge base. Use this skill when adding dysbiosis mechanisms, ecological concepts (Anna Karenina, keystone taxa, colonization resistance), SCFA/metabolite pathways, and linking microbial ecology to disease pathophysiology. Covers IBD, C. diff, obesity, and other microbiome-associated conditions. ([monarch-initiative/dismech](https://github.com/monarch-initiative/dismech/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/microbiome-curation) · ★73)
- **microbiome-cutotype-stratification**: Use when you have a species-level microbial abundance table plus sample metadata with group labels (e.g., cutotypes) and want genus-level enrichment of taxa rather than KEGG pathway/functional enrichment. - **microbiome-dataset-characteristic-computation**: Use when you have per-tool significant ASV outputs for many two-group 16S datasets and want to test whether the numbers of significant features correlate with effect size, sample size, sequencing depth, or sparsity, with vs without prevalence filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-dataset-characteristic-computation))
- **microbiome-differential-abundance**: Differential abundance testing for microbiome data using compositionally-aware methods like ALDEx2, ANCOM-BC2, and MaAsLin2. Use when identifying taxa that differ between experimental groups while accounting for the compositional nature of microbiome data. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/microbiome-differential-abundance) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **microbiome-differential-abundance-analysis**: Use when analyzing microbiome OTU/feature count tables to identify taxa differentially abundant across experimental groups (e.g., nationality, age strata, bmi_group) while accounting for compositionality and unequal sampling fractions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-differential-abundance-analysis))
- **microbiome-differential-abundance-results-aggregation**: Use when you have significant-ASV result tables from several DA tools (e.g., 14 tools) run on the same datasets and need to quantify overlap, consensus, or tool clustering, including effects of prevalence filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-differential-abundance-results-aggregation))
- **microbiome-differential-abundance-testing**: Use when comparing compositional microbiome feature counts between AD model and control cohorts, e.g. testing KEGG pathway abundances in the 3xTg Sacrifice cohort against WT to reproduce Figure 5E. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-differential-abundance-testing))
- **microbiome-differential-abundance-testing**: Use when a binary or continuous grouping factor and a microbiome feature table are available and you need to test differential abundance while quantifying robustness of individual features and the overall microbiome-group association. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-differential-abundance-testing))
- **microbiome-disease-research**: 研究微生物组与疾病机制证据链并形成研究假设。适用于：梳理菌群、代谢物、宿主通路与疾病表型之间的证据链，区分相关性与因果性并形成可验证研究假设。 ([patsnap/skills](https://github.com/patsnap/skills/tree/HEAD/open-platform/microbiome-disease-research) · ★46)
- **microbiome-diversity-analysis**: Alpha and beta diversity analysis for microbiome data. Calculate within-sample richness, evenness, and between-sample dissimilarity with phyloseq and vegan. Use when comparing community composition across samples or testing for group differences in microbiome structure. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/microbiome-diversity-analysis) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **microbiome-diversity-report-component-organization**: Use when you have raw microbiome analysis outputs (e.g., EMP data, alpha diversity, Shannon entropy, network files) that must be assembled into a reproducible structured scientific report. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-diversity-report-component-organization))
- **microbiome-diversity-reporter**: Interpret Alpha and Beta diversity metrics from 16S rRNA sequencing results. ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/scientific-skills/Academic%20Writing/microbiome-diversity-reporter) · ★2.1k · 2 copies)
- **microbiome-diversity-statistics**: Use when a genus-level ASV abundance table is grouped by experimental condition or amplified region and you must test whether alpha diversity (richness/evenness) differs significantly across groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-diversity-statistics))
- **microbiome-effect-size-visualization**: Use when progressive permutation has flagged robust microbiome features (observed -log10 p-values outside the 95% quantile intervals of the fully permuted scenario) and you need signed effect sizes and individual abundance plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-effect-size-visualization))
- **microbiome-environment-stratification**: Use when an EMP 16S Release 1 BIOM table and mapping file must be analyzed per environment type — e.g., computing NODF nestedness separately for non-saline, saline, animal, and plant subsets of a 2,000-sample subset rather than only globally. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-environment-stratification))
- **microbiome-feature-ordering-for-cnn-input**: Use when you have a cophenetic distance matrix from a phylogenetic tree (e.g., from the R ape package) of OTU features and need to reorder/group them phylogenetically before training a CNN-based microbiome classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-feature-ordering-for-cnn-input))
- **microbiome-feature-robustness-ranking**: Use when binary-grouped microbiome count data has yielded significant differential features and you need to rank how robust each discovery is to progressive permutation of grouping labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-feature-robustness-ranking))
- **microbiome-feature-screening**: Use when a high-dimensional OTU/taxa count matrix (e.g. 856 OTUs x 60 samples) and an adjusted response vector must be reduced to a non-empty subset S of taxa likely associated with the outcome, prior to a data-adaptive multivariate association test under a generalized linear model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-feature-screening))
- **microbiome-feature-table-analysis**: Use when an amplicon feature table (BIOM/QIIME 2 format) and a sample metadata mapping file are loaded and you must determine whether experimental groups differ in alpha- or beta-diversity and which features are biomarkers of each group. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-feature-table-analysis))
- **microbiome-feature-table-rarefaction**: Use when a feature table (Deblur or OTU BIOM) with uneven per-sample sequence counts feeds into cross-sample statistics (e.g., random forest classification of EMPO environment labels), and per-sample depths must be equalized to 5,000 observations first. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-feature-table-rarefaction))
- **microbiome-fragility-index-computation**: Use when a set of differentially abundant microbiome features from a binary-group comparison must be ranked by robustness, by counting how many progressive label-permutation steps flip each feature to nonsignificance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-fragility-index-computation))
- **microbiome-functional-data-interpretation**: Use when input is PICRUSt2 predicted functional output (e.g., KO abundance tables and sample metadata) and the goal is differential pathway abundance, pathway annotation, or GSEA between comparison groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-functional-data-interpretation))
- **microbiome-functional-prediction**: Predict metagenome functional content from 16S rRNA marker gene data using PICRUSt2. Infer KEGG, MetaCyc, and EC abundances from ASV tables. Use when functional profiling is needed from 16S data without shotgun metagenomics sequencing. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/microbiome-functional-prediction) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **microbiome-functional-profile-visualization**: Use when you have a DA results table from pathway_daa() applied to PICRUSt2 KEGG pathway abundances and need a publication-quality figure showing which pathways differ between groups and by how much. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-functional-profile-visualization))
- **microbiome-genotype-association-analysis**: Use when you have a SNP genotype matrix and a microbial taxa abundance matrix over the same sample cohort and need to identify significant taxa-SNP (mbQTL) relationships, including all-to-all R² estimates or a single specified rsID. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-genotype-association-analysis))
- **microbiome-group-comparison-design**: Use when you have a PICRUSt2-predicted pathway-level abundance table (KO converted to KEGG pathway abundance) plus sample metadata with a grouping variable, and you must test which pathways differ significantly between groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-group-comparison-design))
- **microbiome-group-difference-analysis**: Use when a microbiome OTU/feature count table has a binary grouping factor and you need to characterize overall association strength and the robustness of significant features across progressive label permutations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-group-difference-analysis))
- **microbiome-host-phenotype-association-analysis**: Use when metagenomic shotgun samples have been profiled with MetaPhlAn 4 and you must link taxonomic abundances (e.g. SGB4777) to host markers like HEI, aMED, uPDI or ASCVD risk via prediction metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-host-phenotype-association-analysis))
- **microbiome-library-size-normalization**: Use when preparing 16S rRNA gene ASV/OTU count tables for differential abundance tools that require rarefied input, or when reproducing the rarefaction-vs-no-rarefaction arm of a DA method comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-library-size-normalization))
- **microbiome-marker-gene-survey-analysis**: Use when the input is a sparse high-throughput microbiome OTU/taxa count matrix with two or more sample groups and the goal is to identify features that are differentially abundant across populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-marker-gene-survey-analysis))
- **microbiome-metabolite-prediction-modeling**: Use when paired samples have matched microbiome (taxonomic or functional abundance) and metabolite count tables and the goal is to predict metabolite abundances, identify well-predicted metabolites, or infer microbe-metabolite interaction networks without relying on metabolite annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-metabolite-prediction-modeling))
- **microbiome-metabolome-abundance-normalization**: Use when when you have raw count matrices from paired microbiome (16S rRNA or metagenomic) and metabolomic (LC-MS/MS) profiling data that will be used to train or apply a predictive model (e.g., MiMeNet, MelonnPan, Random Forest) to predict metabolite abundances from microbial composition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/microbiome-metabolome-abundance-normalization) · 3 copies)
- **microbiome-metabolome-cross-validation**: Use when paired microbial-feature and metabolite tables (samples-by-features CSV) must be evaluated by k-fold cross-validation and a principled significance cutoff for well-predicted metabolites is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-metabolome-cross-validation))
- **microbiome-metabolome-data-preprocessing**: Use when when starting with raw paired microbiome (16S rRNA, metagenomic taxonomic or functional features) and metabolome (LC-MS/MS, NMR) count tables from the same biospecimens, and planning to train prediction models or co-abundance networks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/microbiome-metabolome-data-preprocessing) · 3 copies)
- **microbiome-metabolome-data-preprocessing-clr-transformation**: Use when you have paired microbiome and metabolomic abundance tables (samples × features) with relative abundance or raw count values, and you are preparing data for downstream regression or neural network modeling of microbe-metabolite relationships. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/microbiome-metabolome-data-preprocessing-clr-transformation) · 3 copies)
- **microbiome-metabolome-integrative-analysis**: Use when paired samples have matched taxonomic/functional abundance and metabolite abundance matrices (e.g. IBD PRISM, cystic fibrosis, soil datasets) and you need to predict the metabolome, identify well-predicted metabolites, and group microbes and metabolites with similar interaction patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-metabolome-integrative-analysis))
- **microbiome-metabolome-prediction-modeling**: Use when you have paired microbiome (16S rRNA, metagenomic) and metabolomic (LC-MS, GC-MS) abundance tables from the same biosamples, and you want to predict which metabolites are recoverable from microbial composition alone and identify groups of microbes and metabolites with correlated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/microbiome-metabolome-prediction-modeling) · 4 copies)
- **microbiome-metadata-association-analysis**: Use when you have per-sample inStrain profile objects (or equivalent strain calls) plus per-infant metadata (sibling/twin relationships, birth day, birth weight, gestational age) and need to test whether strain sharing correlates with these host properties. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-metadata-association-analysis))
- **microbiome-metadata-association-modeling**: Use when comparing whether two sequence variant callers yield comparable microbiome-biological-variable association performance, or when building supervised classification models from a variant count table (e.g., SvTable.txt) merged with sample metadata. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-metadata-association-modeling))
- **microbiome-metadata-driven-plot-styling**: Use when you have a QIIME principal coordinates file plus a metadata mapping file and need the ordination points colored, toggled, or made transparent by metadata categories or gradients in a 3D web visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-metadata-driven-plot-styling))
- **microbiome-metadata-mapping-file-handling**: Use when you have QIIME principal coordinates files plus a metadata mapping file and need to color, recolor, or toggle visibility of PCoA points by metadata categories or gradients in an interactive 3D plot. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-metadata-mapping-file-handling))
- **microbiome-metadata-mapping-integration**: Use when you have QIIME PCoA coordinates and a metadata mapping file and need an interactive, embeddable 3D ordination plot (Chrome/WebGL) shared via email or a web page. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-metadata-mapping-integration))
- **microbiome-metadata-matching**: Use when a merged MetaPhlAn or QIIME OTU/BIOM profile table must be paired with a tab-separated metadata file whose class column (e.g. column 7, T1D_Diagnosed with t/f labels) and sample ID column (e.g. column 2, Gid_shotgun) must be identified before MegaR model building. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-metadata-matching))
- **microbiome-method-comparison-benchmarking**: Use when benchmarking differential-abundance methods for zero-inflated microbiome relative abundance data, e.g. to quantify empirical power of GAMLSS-BEZI vs. transformed linear models across effect sizes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-method-comparison-benchmarking))
- **microbiome-metric-aggregation-and-comparison**: Use when per-dataset alpha diversity metric tables from QIIME2's diversity module must be merged with metadata into one table, or when metric conclusions must be re-validated under a second condition such as rarefaction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-metric-aggregation-and-comparison))
- **microbiome-mock-control-validation**: Use when amplicon ASV abundance tables (e.g., Deblur-derived, genus-level) must be cross-validated against a mock community standard such as ZymoBIOMICS to quantify classifier sensitivity/specificity via ROC/AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-mock-control-validation))
- **microbiome-module-feature-engineering**: Use when you have paired microbiome-metabolite data and want to replace thousands of original abundance features with a smaller set of module feature values (mean normalized abundance per biclustered or WGCNA-derived module) for a classification task such as IBD status prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-module-feature-engineering))
- **microbiome-multiple-testing-correction**: Decision support for taxon-level association testing on microbiome abundance tables (e.g. species-level ANCOM-BC2 output) where multiple hypotheses are tested and raw p-values must be adjusted before reporting differentially abundant taxa. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-multiple-testing-correction))
- **microbiome-otu-compositional-normalization**: Use when the input is a raw OTU abundance count matrix (e.g. 16S V1-V2 OTUs from QIIME) that must be compositional-normalized and OTU-wise standardized before feature screening or multivariate association testing in the AMAT framework. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-otu-compositional-normalization))
- **microbiome-otu-data-simulation**: Use when you must benchmark type I error calibration or statistical power of a microbiome multivariate association test against synthetic OTU count data generated to mimic a real community. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-otu-data-simulation))
- **microbiome-phylogeny-aware-abundance-profiling**: Use when you have feature/OTU tables from multiple denoising or clustering methods (e.g. vsearch open/closed-reference, de novo, DADA2, Deblur) and need to assess how similarly they resolve community composition using phylogeny-aware beta-diversity metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-phylogeny-aware-abundance-profiling))
- **microbiome-pipeline**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia/tree/HEAD/Skills/Workflow_Management/workflows/microbiome-pipeline) · ★7)

- **microbiome-pipeline-gptomics-bioskills-2**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. - **microbiome-pipeline-phase-tool-mapping**: Use when building or auditing a 16S rRNA or shotgun metagenomics pipeline and you need to know which concrete tool (e.g. Trimmomatic, MEGAHIT, MetaBAT, Kraken2, Prokka, mothur, DADA2) the -practice literature assigns to each phase. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-pipeline-phase-tool-mapping))
- **microbiome-pvalue-trace-interpretation**: Use when a progressive permutation run (bin_permute_all output on a binary grouping outcome) needs interpretation via U-curves and p-value traces to assess overall microbiome association strength. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-pvalue-trace-interpretation))
- **microbiome-qiime2-workflow**: QIIME2 command-line workflow for 16S/ITS amplicon analysis. Alternative to DADA2/phyloseq R workflow with built-in provenance tracking. Use when preferring CLI over R, needing reproducible provenance, or working within QIIME2 ecosystem. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/microbiome-qiime2-workflow) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **microbiome-qtl-preprocessing**: Use when a taxa-by-sample 16S abundance table (plus an optional SNP/covariate file) must be prepared for mbQTL rho estimation, and taxa–taxa R values need to be estimated as inputs to taxa–SNP association analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-qtl-preprocessing))
- **microbiome-quality-control-filtering**: Use when raw OTU count tables and taxonomy tables (e.g., otu.tab.txt / tax.tab.txt from phyloseq) must be quality-controlled before survival-microbiome analysis and you need MiSurv's default filtering criteria reproduced. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-quality-control-filtering))
- **microbiome-relabundance-null-data-generation**: Use when evaluating the false positive control of a differential abundance test on zero-inflated beta relative abundance data — e.g. simulating null data with mu1=mu2=0.5, alpha1=alpha2=0.5, phi=5 to reproduce Table 1 type I error rates at alpha=0.01/0.05/0.1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-relabundance-null-data-generation))
- **microbiome-relative-abundance-modeling**: Use when testing differential relative abundance of microbial taxa (phylum to genus, e.g. levels l2/l4/l5/l6) between groups in compositional, zero-inflated 16S microbiome data, especially longitudinal designs with covariate adjustment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-relative-abundance-modeling))
- **microbiome-research**: Analyze microbiome and metagenomics data using MGnify, GTDB, ENA, and literature tools. Search studies by biome/keyword, retrieve taxonomic profiles and functional annotations, classify genomes with GTDB taxonomy, and find related publications. Use for human gut microbiome, soil/ocean metagenomics, and environmental microbiology research. ([mindspore-ai/mindscience](https://github.com/mindspore-ai/mindscience/tree/HEAD/MindScienceSkills/biology/genomics_and_genetics/microbiome-research) · ★56)
- **microbiome-sample-grouping-by-hypervariable-region**: Use when genus-level collapsed ASV abundance tables (e.g., Greengenes-assigned, QIIME 2-produced) must be grouped by 16S hypervariable region to assess compositional dissimilarity in ordination space between region combinations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-sample-grouping-by-hypervariable-region))
- **microbiome-sample-metadata-extraction**: Use when a microbiome survival analysis needs clinical/design variables (survival time, event indicator, treatment group, covariates) pulled from a sample-data table such as MiSurv's sam.dat.txt or the phyloseq object biom.Rdata. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-sample-metadata-extraction))
- **microbiome-sample-metadata-stratification**: Use when a denoised QIIME 2 feature table and metadata columns (e.g., location, size fraction) are available and the goal is to determine if 16S communities are significantly distinguished by those grouping factors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-sample-metadata-stratification))
- **microbiome-scientist**: Think and work like an expert Microbiome Scientist. Use when a task calls for Microbiome Scientist judgment. Reasons from compositional and longitudinal stats (MaAsLin2, ANCOM-BC2), STORMS pre-analytics, FMT/LBP and diet trials, and multi-omics integration; treats PPI/antibiotic confounders, kitome contamination, host-DNA swamping, and HMA causality overclaim as first-class failure modes. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/tree/HEAD/scientific-agents/microbiome-scientist/skills/microbiome-scientist) · ★255 · 2 copies)
- **microbiome-scientist**: Expert-thinking profile for Microbiome Scientist (cohort / intervention / multi-omics host–microbiome): Reasons from compositional and longitudinal stats (MaAsLin2, ANCOM- BC2), STORMS pre-analytics, FMT/LBP and diet trials, and multi-omics integration; treats PPI/antibiotic confounders, kitome contamination, host-DNA swamping, and HMA causality overclaim as first-class failure modes. ([stanfish06/skillquarium](https://github.com/stanfish06/skillquarium/tree/HEAD/skills/microbiome-scientist) · ★13)
- **microbiome-scientist**: Reasons from compositional and longitudinal stats (MaAsLin2, ANCOM-BC2), STORMS pre-analytics, FMT/LBP and diet trials, and multi-omics integration; treats PPI/antibiotic confounders, kitome contamination, host-DNA swamping, and HMA causality overclaim as first-class failure modes. ([raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft](https://github.com/raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft/tree/HEAD/skills/microbiome-scientist) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **microbiome-scientist**: Expert instructions and domain workflows for microbiome scientist. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/engineering/microbiome-scientist))
- **microbiome-sequence-count-tabulation**: Use when raw 16S rRNA amplicon FASTQ files (e.g., 33 sputum samples sequenced on Illumina MiSeq) must be quality-filtered, denoised, and reduced to per-sample sequence counts, per-region ASV totals, and genus-level taxonomy counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-sequence-count-tabulation))
- **microbiome-simulation-benchmarking**: Use when comparing power/type I error of multivariate microbiome association tests on simulated OTU count data with continuous or binary outcomes, e.g. Dirichlet-multinomial scenarios varying signal density. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-simulation-benchmarking))
- **microbiome-simulation-data-generation**: Use when a study needs controlled synthetic OTU/count data — e.g., known sampling fractions, 500–1000 taxa, unbalanced microbial load — to test bias correction, normalization residuals, or CPU-time scaling of DA methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-simulation-data-generation))
- **microbiome-source-tracking-simulation**: Use when evaluating or comparing source-tracking algorithms on compositional data with many candidate sources (e.g., M=50 environments, K=10 true contributors) and substantial unknown-source mixing (0–90%), or when validating that a new MST method actually reduces noise from noncontributing sources. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-source-tracking-simulation))
- **microbiome-taxa-abundance-difference-modeling**: Use when you have a pre-processed taxa-by-sample absolute abundance table plus group assignments and need to estimate/correct the sampling-fraction bias (delta) via the ANCOM-BC E-M mixture model before testing taxon differential abundance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbiome-taxa-abundance-difference-modeling))
- **microbiome-taxonomic-cladogram-annotation**: Use when you have MetaPhlAn or LEfSe biomarker results (e.g., 16S rRNA profiles from hmp_aerobiosis) and need a compact, publication-quality circular phylogeny with clade-level biomarker highlighting and shaded backgrounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-taxonomic-cladogram-annotation))
- **microbiome-taxonomy-assignment**: Taxonomic classification of ASVs using reference databases like SILVA, GTDB, or UNITE. Covers naive Bayes classifiers (DADA2, IDTAXA) and exact matching approaches. Use when assigning taxonomy to ASVs after DADA2 amplicon processing. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/microbiome-taxonomy-assignment) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **microbiome-temporal-dynamics-visualization**: Use when the input is a BIOM (version 1 or 2) abundance matrix from a 16S rRNA sequencing study (e.g., vaginal microbiota temporal-dynamics data) and a compact circular phylogenetic/taxonomy visualization with highlighted biomarkers is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-temporal-dynamics-visualization))
- **microbiome-therapist**: Prompt playbook for a microbiome therapist. Use when a microbiome therapist wants help with work tasks such as: translate a microbiome test report; recurrent C. diff therapy options brief; donor screening protocol gap check; personalized diet-microbiome plan; probiotic claim fact-check; informed consent and patient handout; adverse event and outcomes log review; field upskilling plan. ([avivelbag/future-career-prompt-skills](https://github.com/avivelbag/future-career-prompt-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/microbiome-therapist))
- **microbiome-time-series-trajectory-prediction**: Use when longitudinal OTU count tables (taxa x samples with sequence IDs and sample times) have been denoised by LUMINATE 'estimate', and the goal is to infer cLV parameters (A, g, B) and predict future community trajectories under external perturbations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbiome-time-series-trajectory-prediction))
- **microbiome-visualization-load-benchmarking**: Use when a PCoA/beta-diversity visualization of thousands of microbiome samples loads too slowly or produces unwieldy plot files, and you need quantitative load-time and file-size evidence to choose between visualization tools such as EMPeror and KiNG. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiome-visualization-load-benchmarking))
- **Microbiome_Cancer_Agent**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia/tree/HEAD/Skills/Microbiome/Microbiome_Cancer_Agent) · ★7)
- **microbiome_genomics**: Microbiome Genomics Analysis - Analyze microbial genome: NCBI genome data, taxonomy, KEGG metabolic pathways, and annotation. Use this skill for metagenomics tasks involving get genome dataset report by taxon get taxonomy kegg find get genome annotation report. Combines 4 tools from 2 SCP server(s). ([SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw](https://github.com/SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/microbiome_genomics) · ★399 · 4 copies)
- **microbiorel-re-eval**: Evaluates generative and discriminative models on document-level relation extraction in the microbiome domain. It probes the ability to correctly classify pairwise relations between biomedical entities (species, diseases, chemicals, etc.) under a low-resource setting. Use when the user wants to benchmark on MicrobioRel, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Weighted F1-score. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/microbiorel-re-eval) · ★3)
- **microbiota-flux-aggregation**: Use when a human GEM (e.g. RECON3D in SBML/cobrapy format) must be conditioned on gut microbiota metabolism — i.e. when studying metabolic interaction between gut microbes and human cells and exchange-reaction lower bounds need updating from microbial fluxes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiota-flux-aggregation))
- **microbiota-metabolic-model-parsing**: Use when you have a gut microbiota metabolic model (SBML) and need its per-metabolite boundary fluxes aggregated and normalized (scaled to e.g. 1000) to modify exchange-reaction lower bounds in a human GEM like RECON3D. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiota-metabolic-model-parsing))
- **microbiota-metabolite-mapping-to-host-gem**: Use when a human GEM (e.g., RECON3D) must be modified with flux information from gut microbiota models, updating exchange-reaction lower bounds of reactions that contain metabolites present in the microbiota model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbiota-metabolite-mapping-to-host-gem))
- **microbit**: Use when writing code or wiring designs for the BBC micro:bit (V1 or V2) — edge connector projects, motor drivers, sensors, radio, or breakout boards. Provides V1/V2 hardware differences, full edge-connector pinout, LED-matrix pin conflicts, power budget limits, radio group behaviour, and MicroPython vs MakeCode tradeoffs.
